                                                    <p class="card-title">About Library</p>

                                                <hr class="bg-gradient-success" style="height: 1px; width: 100%; margin: 0px; border-top: 0px; padding: 0px;" />

                                                <p style="text-align: left; font-family: Arial;">
                                                    <span id="Lbl_Lib_Intro">It is Polytechnic Library in West Bengal,started in 1998. The vission of this library is to became model Polytechnic library among the Polytechnic Library of West Bengal.It intent to incorporate the latest technology and adopt user friendly approach towards the students and faculty.Library intents to offer comprehensive services related to disseminates of knowledge.The mission of this library is to provide users with the information they need to achieve their highest academic protential and help them acquire skill necessary for life long learning.</span><br />
                                                    <span id="Lbl_Lib_Intro2">এই পলিটেকনিক স্থাপিত হয়েছিল ১৯৯৮ সালে I পলিটেকনিকের মূল লক্ষ্য হল আধুনিক প্রযুক্তি ব্যবহার করে লাইব্রেরি রিসোর্স কে সর্বাধিক ব্যবহার যোগ্য করে তোলা, ব্যবহার কারী দের কাছে I সেই লক্ষ্য নিয়ে eg4 ব্যবহার করা হচ্ছে I</span>
                                                </p>

                                                <p style="text-align: left; font-family: Arial;">
                                                    <span id="Lbl_Lib_Objective">The objectives of this library is to provide library resources and services in multiple format that meet our users need. To educate users about the library services and resources.To increase collaboration with students, faculty and staff community.</span><br />
                                                    <span id="Lbl_Lib_Objective2">সঠিক তথ্য সঠিক সময়ে সঠিক ব্যবহারকারীকে দেয়া</span>
                                                </p>

                                                <p style="text-align: left; font-family: Arial;">

                                    <div class="col-md-6 grid-margin stretch-card">
                                        <div class="card">
                                            <div class="card-body">
                                                <div class="d-flex justify-content-start">
                                                    <p class="card-title">Library Rules</p>
                                                </div>
                                                <hr class="bg-gradient-warning" style="height: 1px; width: 100%; margin: 0px; border-top: 0px; padding: 0px;" />
                                                <hr class="bg-light" style="height: 1px; width: 100%; margin: 0px; border-top: 0px; padding: 0px;" />
                                                <p style="text-align: left;">
                                                    <span id="Lbl_Lib_Rules">Nazrul Centenary Polytechnic Library Rules and Regulations:-<br /><br />General Rules<br /><br />1.	All students and staff of the Polytechnic are members of the library.<br />2.	Registration should be done to become a library member prior to using the library resources <br />3.	Library Card is compulsory for getting access to the library<br />4.	Enter your name and Sign in the register kept at the entrance counter before entering library<br />5.	Drink and food are not allowed in the Library. They must keep their bags outside the library before entering.<br />6.	Students can borrow books for a period of Six Months (180 days) in this way:-<br />A.	Semester 1st only two books,<br />B. Semester 2nd only three books, <br />C. Semester 3rd& 4th only four books, and <br />D. Semester 5th& 6th only 5 books<br />Extra book can be allowed on the decision of Librarian. <br />7.	Internal Members can borrow books for one year in this way:-<br />A. Lecturers may borrow maximum 20 books.<br />B. Non-Lecturers may borrow maximum 10 books.<br />C. Admin may borrow maximum 5 books<br />8.	Books will be issued to the students, on the basis of library notice. <br />9.	Textbooks, printed materials and issued books are not allowed to be taken inside the library<br />10.	 Marking, underlining or writing on library books, periodicals are strictly forbidden.<br />11.	 Reference books and periodicals will not be issued to any member. These can be consulted only in the library.<br />12.	 If the books are not returned within the specified time it will be viewed seriously and fine will be charged.<br />13.	The Librarian may call for a book at any time, even if the normal period of loan has not expired.<br />14.	 In case of book is misused, wrongly handled or lost the person concerned will have to replace.<br />15.	The members should take good care of library Documents.<br />16.	Drink and food are not allowed in the Library. They must keep their bags outside the library before enteri<br />17.	The library is a “Silence Zone”. Students have to strictly maintain silence in the library.<br />18.	No discussion permitted inside the library<br />19.	Each student has to show his/her book at the exit during dispersal.<br />20.	For loss or damage of the library book, the student has to replace the same within 1 week.<br />21.	Using Mobile phones and audio instruments with or without speaker or headphone is strictly prohibited in the library premises<br />22.	Library borrower cards are not transferable. The borrower is responsible for the books borrowed on his/her card.<br />23.	Each student has to check his or her library book during issue and report impairment or any damage to the librarian. If any scribbling or damage to the book is found at the time of return then he /she will have to replace the book.<br />24.	Refreshment of any kind shall not be taken anywhere in the library premises.<br /><br />Working Hours of the Library:<br /><br />Monday to Friday	10.30 am to 4pm <br />Saturday	10.30 am to1.00 pm<br />	<br /><br /><br />Circulation <br />Books will be issued on presentation of the library card. Students are instructed to check the books while borrowing and they will be responsible for any type of damage or mutilation noticed at the time of return.<br /> <br />Overdue Charges: <br />Materials borrowed should be returned on or before the due date stamped, if returned late overdue fine will be charged for the delayed period.<br /> Slab for Fine Collection:<br />After 180 days 	Rs.1/- per day<br /><br />Book Lost: <br />If the books are lost, then the borrower shall replace the books of the same edition or latest edition.<br /> <br />Care of library borrower cards: <br />Take special care to maintain the library borrower cards. Do not fold, alter entries made on the cards, Members are responsible for the entire set of library borrower card issued to them.<br /> <br />Loss of Library cards: <br />Loss of borrower card should be reported to the librarian. Duplicate card may be issued against formal application and fine will be charged Rupees 20 only.<br /> <br />Validity of cards: <br />Library borrower cards are valid for the entire duration of the course to access library facilities. At the end of the course borrower cards shall be returned to the library.<br /> <br />No due Certificate: <br />Each student shall obtain No dues certificate from the library after returning all the books issued, surrendering the borrower’s cards and after paying outstanding dues, if any.<br /> <br /><br />Reference section: <br />This section has Atlas, Dictionaries, Textbooks, Reference books etc. which are only available for reference. User can make use of these resources.<br /> <br />Journal Section: <br />In these section journals are available. They are arranged alphabetically. The latest issues are displayed on display rack and other previous issues are arranged in the drawer. Bound volumes of periodicals are arranged in rack alphabetically and are meant only for reference.</span>
                                                </p>
